### Community 106 - "Phone Numbers; Communication Tools Culture Note"
Cohesion: 0.12
Nodes (22): Cell phone number format (11 digits, 3-4-4 pause pattern, '1' read as yāo), Culture Note: Common Communication Tools of Chinese People, 在中国，常用的通信工具有电话机和手机两种。 (Zài Zhōngguó, chángyòng de tōngxìn gōngjù yǒu diànhuàjī hé shǒujī liǎng zhǒng.) - In China, there are two kinds of common communication tools—telephones and cell phones., 电话机号码一般为7-8位，比如2027816、82304156，不同地区的电话机号码位数不同。 (Diànhuàjī hàomǎ yìbān wéi 7-8 wèi, bǐrú 2027816, 82304156, bùtóng dìqū de diànhuàjī hàomǎ wèishù bùtóng.) - A telephone number usually has 7-8 digits, for example, 2027816, 82304156. Telephone numbers in different regions have different numbers of digits., 手机号码的位数没有地区的差别，都是11位，比如13576983311。 (Shǒujī hàomǎ de wèishù méiyǒu dìqū de chābié, dōu shì 11 wèi, bǐrú 13576983311.) - Cell phone numbers, however, always have 11 digits regardless of region, as in 13576983311., 由于手机号码位数较多，读时一般切分为“3-4-4”的停顿模式，比如139-0107-8866。 (Yóuyú shǒujī hàomǎ wèishù jiào duō, dú shí yìbān qiēfēn wéi “3-4-4” de tíngdùn móshì, bǐrú 139-0107-8866.) - Since a cell phone number has many digits, it is read with pauses following the pattern "3-4-4", such as 139-0107-8866., 其中号码中的“1”要读成“yāo”。 (Qízhōng hàomǎ zhōng de “1” yào dúchéng “yāo”.) - "1" is read as "yāo" in a phone number., Telephone number format (7-8 digits, region-dependent) (+14 more)

### Community 181 - "HSK Course Design and Features"
Cohesion: 0.17
Nodes (15): Communicative Exercises for HSK Oral Test, HSK Course Curriculum Table (9 Volumes), Course feature 3: Separate communicative exercises closely integrated with HSK oral exam content (单独设置交际练习，紧密结合HSK口试内容), Course feature 1: Written level by level based on the HSK Syllabus (以汉语水平考试大纲为依据，逐级编写"教程"), Course feature 2: Each volume has a workbook whose exercise forms match HSK question types (每册教材配有练习册，练习册中练习的形式与HSK题型吻合), 内容的多样性和丰富性，一般来说，一个主题确定后，通过接触和这个主题相关的多个方面的学习内容，加速学生对新内容的内化和理解，进而深入探究，培养学生的创造能力。"教程"为了联系学生的实际，开阔学生的视野，从四级分册开始以主题引领，每个主题下又分为若干小主题，主题之间相互联系形成有机的知识网络，使之牢固地镶嵌在学生的记忆深处，不易遗忘。 (Nèiróng de duōyàngxìng hé fēngfùxìng, yībān lái shuō, yī gè zhǔtí quèdìng hòu, tōngguò jiēchù hé zhège zhǔtí xiāngguān de duō gè fāngmiàn de xuéxí nèiróng, jiāsù xuéshēng duì xīn nèiróng de nèihuà hé lǐjiě, jìn'ér shēnrù tànjiū, péiyǎng xuéshēng de chuàngzào nénglì. "Jiàochéng" wèile liánxì xuéshēng de shíjì, kāikuò xuéshēng de shìyě, cóng sì jí fēncè kāishǐ yǐ zhǔtí yǐnlǐng, měi gè zhǔtí xià yòu fēnwéi ruògān xiǎo zhǔtí, zhǔtí zhījiān xiānghù liánxì xíngchéng yǒujī de zhīshì wǎngluò, shǐ zhī láogù de xiāngqiàn zài xuéshēng de jìyì shēnchù, bùyì yíwàng.) - Once a topic is fixed, exposure to multiple aspects of learning content related to it accelerates students' internalization and understanding of new content, leads to deeper exploration, and cultivates creative ability; starting from the fourth-level volumes the Course is led by themes, each divided into sub-themes, interconnected into an organic network of knowledge firmly embedded in students' memory., 汉语水平考试（HSK）共分六个等级，"教程"编教人员仔细研读了"大纲"和出题指南，并对大量真题进行了统计、分析。根据真题统计结果归纳出每册的重点、难点、语言点、话题、功能、场景等，在遵循HSK大纲词汇要求的前提下，系统设计了各级别的范围、课时等，具体安排如下： (Hànyǔ Shuǐpíng Kǎoshì (HSK) gòng fēn liù gè děngjí, "jiàochéng" biānjiào rényuán zǐxì yándúle "dàgāng" hé chūtí zhǐnán, bìng duì dàliàng zhēntí jìnxíngle tǒngjì, fēnxī. Gēnjù zhēntí tǒngjì jiéguǒ guīnà chū měi cè de zhòngdiǎn, nándiǎn, yǔyándiǎn, huàtí, gōngnéng, chǎngjǐng děng, zài zūnxún HSK dàgāng cíhuì yāoqiú de qiántí xià, xìtǒng shèjìle gè jíbié de fànwéi, kèshí děng, jùtǐ ānpái rúxià:) - The HSK is divided into six levels; the editors studied the Syllabus and question-writing guidelines and statistically analysed many real exam questions, summarising each volume's key points, difficulties, language points, topics, functions and scenarios, and systematically designed the scope and class hours of each level within the HSK Syllabus vocabulary requirements, as follows:, 这种设计遵循汉语国际教育的理念，注重教材的普适性、应用性和实用性，海内外教学机构可根据学时建议来设计每册书完成的年限。比如，一级的《教程1》规定用34学时完成，如果国内周课时是8课时的话，大概一个月左右就能学完；在海外如果一周是4课时的话，就需要两个月的时间能学完。以此类推。一般来说，学完《教程1》就能通过一级考试，同样学完《教程2》就能通过二级考试，等等。 (Zhè zhǒng shèjì zūnxún Hànyǔ guójì jiàoyù de lǐniàn, zhùzhòng jiàocái de pǔshìxìng, yìngyòngxìng hé shíyòngxìng, hǎinèiwài jiàoxué jīgòu kě gēnjù xuéshí jiànyì lái shèjì měi cè shū wánchéng de niánxiàn. Bǐrú, yī jí de "Jiàochéng 1" guīdìng yòng 34 xuéshí wánchéng, rúguǒ guónèi zhōu kèshí shì 8 kèshí dehuà, dàgài yī gè yuè zuǒyòu jiù néng xuéwán; zài hǎiwài rúguǒ yī zhōu shì 4 kèshí dehuà, jiù xūyào liǎng gè yuè de shíjiān néng xuéwán. Yǐ cǐ lèituī. Yībān lái shuō, xuéwán "Jiàochéng 1" jiù néng tōngguò yī jí kǎoshì, tóngyàng xuéwán "Jiàochéng 2" jiù néng tōngguò èr jí kǎoshì, děngděng.) - The design follows the philosophy of international Chinese education; institutions can set the completion time per volume from the recommended class hours. For example, Course 1 is designed for 34 class hours: about one month at 8 hours per week domestically, two months at 4 hours per week abroad. Generally, finishing Course 1 lets one pass the Level 1 exam, finishing Course 2 the Level 2 exam, and so on. (+7 more)

Nodes (13): 这句话告诉我们，开始做事情的时候遇到困难是正常的，不要因此而放弃，要坚持下去，以后会越做越容易，越来越好。 (Zhè jù huà gàosu wǒmen, kāishǐ zuò shìqing de shíhou yùdào kùnnan shì zhèngcháng de, bú yào yīncǐ ér fàngqì, yào jiānchí xiàqu, yǐhòu huì yuè zuò yuè róngyì, yuè lái yuè hǎo.) - This saying tells us that meeting difficulties at the start is normal; do not give up because of them but persevere, and it will get easier and better., Common Saying: 一步走错步步错 (One wrong move makes all moves wrong), 一步走错步步错。 (Yí bù zǒucuò bùbù cuò.) - One wrong move makes all moves wrong., "一步走错步步错"的意思是，下棋的时候，如果有一步走错了，那么以后的每一步都是错的。 ("Yí bù zǒucuò bùbù cuò" de yìsi shì, xià qí de shíhou, rúguǒ yǒu yí bù zǒucuò le, nàme yǐhòu de měi yí bù dōu shì cuò de.) - It means that in a chess game, if one move is wrong, then every later move will be wrong too., 这句话用来比喻如果我们开始时做了一个错误的决定，那么以后的每一次决定都会是错的。 (Zhè jù huà yònglái bǐyù rúguǒ wǒmen kāishǐ shí zuòle yí ge cuòwù de juédìng, nàme yǐhòu de měi yí cì juédìng dōu huì shì cuò de.) - Metaphorically, it means that if we make a wrong decision at the start, every decision after it will be wrong., 这句话告诉我们，做事情的时候每一步都要谨慎，避免出现错误，尤其是开始的时候。 (Zhè jù huà gàosu wǒmen, zuò shìqing de shíhou měi yí bù dōu yào jǐnshèn, bìmiǎn chūxiàn cuòwù, yóuqí shì kāishǐ de shíhou.) - This saying tells us to be prudent at every step, to avoid mistakes, especially at the beginning., Common Saying: 站得高，看得远 (The higher you stand, the farther you'll see), 站得高，看得远。 (Zhàn de gāo, kàn de yuǎn.) - The higher you stand, the farther you'll see. (+5 more)

### Community 232 - "Chinese Idioms and Their Stories"
Cohesion: 0.18
Nodes (12): “五十步笑百步”这句话来源于中国古代的一个故事。 ("Wǔshí bù xiào bǎi bù" zhè jù huà láiyuán yú Zhōngguó gǔdài de yí gè gùshi.) - The saying "Wǔshí bù xiào bǎi bù" originates from a story of ancient China., 从前有两个士兵，打仗失败往回跑，一个人跑了五十步，另一个跑了一百步，跑了五十步的就笑话跑了一百步的，说他胆小。 (Cóngqián yǒu liǎng gè shìbīng, dǎzhàng shībài wǎng huí pǎo, yí gè rén pǎole wǔshí bù, lìng yí gè pǎole yìbǎi bù, pǎole wǔshí bù de jiù xiàohua pǎole yìbǎi bù de, shuō tā dǎnxiǎo.) - Once there were two soldiers who fled from a lost battle; one ran 50 steps, the other 100, and the one who ran 50 steps laughed at the one who ran 100, saying he was a coward., 其实两人都是逃跑，只是跑得远近不同。 (Qíshí liǎng rén dōu shì táopǎo, zhǐshì pǎo de yuǎn jìn bù tóng.) - In fact both were running away; the only difference was how far they ran., 这句话用来批评那些跟别人有一样的缺点，却因程度轻而笑话别人的人。 (Zhè jù huà yònglái pīpíng nàxiē gēn biérén yǒu yíyàng de quēdiǎn, què yīn chéngdù qīng ér xiàohua biérén de rén.) - This saying is used to criticize people who have the same fault as others but laugh at them because theirs is milder., “药到病除”的意思是吃了药，病马上就好了，形容医生的医术很好，用药恰到好处。 ("Yàodào bìngchú" de yìsi shì chīle yào, bìng mǎshàng jiù hǎo le, xíngróng yīshēng de yīshù hěn hǎo, yòng yào qiàdào hǎochù.) - "药到病除" means that as soon as medicine is taken the disease is cured, describing a skilful doctor who prescribes just the right medicine., 现在也比喻人能够找到问题的关键，马上解决问题。 (Xiànzài yě bǐyù rén nénggòu zhǎodào wèntí de guānjiàn, mǎshàng jiějué wèntí.) - Now it is also a metaphor for one's ability to find the crux of a problem and solve it immediately., 喜欢喝咖啡了。 (Xǐhuan hē kāfēi le.) - (I) like drinking coffee now., 万事开头难。 (Wàn shì kāitóu nán.) - The first step is always the hardest. (+4 more)

(+1 more)

### Community 286 - "HSK 3 习惯 and 成 Saying"
Cohesion: 0.54
